- `hmh07-lua-follow1:106` (original lines 1039-1050, 1039-1050): GetCurrentSeasonTier requests CoDShared.Loot.GetCurrentSeason(), passes its result with f37_arg0 to opaque Engine hash_6159D7050715A2C3 and returns the Engine result when truthy, or0 when falsey, if reached and the preceding accesses/calls succeed. The result schema and season-tier meaning remain unresolved without the provider contract. TierSkip getters create controller TierSkip then notifyVisible child. InitInGameContractRecord resets owner's _activeContracts, populates daily slot and two pinned slots from model tables; valid contract models initialize id/progressStart/progressEnd/completed(false) and map by id, absent slots receive zero/false fields. Update reads notify data id/progress and writes matching active progressEnd when present, then always refreshes active-slot models. RecordCompleted sets matching completed true when mapped, without adding an absent contract. Server progress/contract identity/data schemas/native model persistence remain external.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:135` (original lines 726-742, 769-776, 769-776, 726-742): Sets notification container alpha to zero when shoutcaster player notifications are disabled or either demo hidden/moviecam visibility bit is set, otherwise one. Uses the event controller for shoutcaster checks and container controller for visibility bits. Ignores notifications after game end unless play-of-match is set. Assigns configured priority, scans the queue until a lower numeric priority or same-type comparator says insert earlier, inserts at that index and immediately calls ShowNextNotification. Equal priorities otherwise retain the traversal order. Reads scoreInfoIndex, gets medal data, creates a streamed image, tracks it in streamedImages, configures image streaming and binds ready/timeout callbacks. Ready removes tracking and queues a Medal holding the image; timeout removes tracking and closes it. The literal timeout/configuration values and image fields are preserved; no successful streaming is assumed. Removes the first displayed widget and passes it to DestroyNotification, then immediately requests ShowNextNotification. Empty-list behavior is not separately guarded. Returns for the explicit trials-HUD gate, nonempty BGB queue or ShouldShowNewNotification false; the latter sets the empty model true only with no displayed widgets. Otherwise marks nonempty and dequeues one notification. It can combine with the last nonexpired item, reset its timer and return. Otherwise closes any streamed medal image, takes a pooled model, calls the type-selected creator, builds a container and timer and appends it. Existing widgets move/scale/fade in reverse order. Excess queue size closes the oldest timer, starts fade and binds completion. Finally schedules queue_done_animating and sets queueAnimating. Missing type-table providers and callback delivery remain separate boundaries. Closes pending streamed medal images, replaces pending queue and marks its model empty, destroys each displayed notification, clears that list, closes all tracked streamed-image keys and clears tracking. No guard, rollback or timer internals are invented. Stores controller/menu, sets a NotificationRewardQueue model and creates RewardMaxRewards+1 pooled child models. Initializes displayed/pending arrays and stores addReward; invocation normalizes CallingCard size to nil, appends fields and calls RewardShowNextItem. queue_done_animating clears the flag and pumps. time_up skips fading items, otherwise plays a TimeUp clip and binds clip_over or fades alpha and binds transition completion. Installation is distinct from reward delivery.

In emblem editor with selected emblem model/index returns the opaque emblem-change query; otherwise falls through to selected paintjob and returns its change query when slot exists. No missing-node or upload-success guard is present.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:046` (original lines 923-936, 923-936): Initializes six controller progress/status models only when absent, then creates dynent/heal/revive/being-revived/bleedout progress and text/prompt children. Healing requires powerRatio>0, ability in-use and player not dead; Reviving uses reviveProgress>0, BeingRevived equality1, CowardsWayOut exact setting plus last-stand/not-ended/not-finished guards, Deposit both dynent progress and depositing>0. This consumes combat state and does not implement revive/deposit timing or native cancellation.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:142` (original lines 1034-1037, 1034-1037, 1034-1037, 1034-1037, 1034-1037, 1034-1037, 1034-1037, 1034-1037): Compares numeric input against numeric Warzone maximum rank plus one. At/above threshold returns the special localized string; otherwise tostring(input). Missing numeric conversions are not guarded. Requires item model not dummy/collapsed. Reads selected slot and item id. If a slot-claiming pickup would replace a nonempty item, uses firstFreeSlot when not -1 or sends inventory_drop first when full. Then sends multi_item_pickup or multi_item_pickup_stowed_weapon with pickup id and chosen slot. These are clientscript requests; no authoritative inventory result is shown. Reads WarzoneSplitInventory.current and forwards controller/item/inventory_drop/amount to SendInventoryNotify. Errors if parent and child are identical. Stores parent&#91;field&#93; as a zero-argument closure; invocation calls child&#91;field&#93;() only if nonnil. It deliberately passes no implicit child receiver, and does not prove the dynamic field provider. Requires showPickupHint. Rejects INVENTORY_FULL, IGNORES_INVENTORY or AMMO_FULL flags. Uses forced-hint item index unless -1, then first free slot unless -1, then selected index or 0. Returns true only if that inventory item model equals the supplied widget model; otherwise false. Reads controller client number and both client team fields. Returns the literal short-circuit conjunction ending in team equality; missing team fields can return nil rather than Boolean false. Missing target platoon returns false. Otherwise returns false for platoonCount=0 or mismatched controller platoon; true for the remaining path. Controller clientModel:get() is not separately guarded. If same controller team, returns teammateIndex or 0 when falsy. Other teams return -1. Gets teammate index; -1 returns empty string, otherwise concatenates empty string with index+1 under Lua operator precedence. Uses TeamPlayerColorNameForClientNum to index ColorSet, then forwards that table to ConvertTableToRGB. Returns the second argument when the third equals 1; otherwise third times first. It does not inspect or move a death circle. Trial sessions localize the special tier-skip key; other sessions localize the supplied key. Reads old mapZoom. Only requested zoom <= MAP_ZOOM_MAX proceeds; there is no lower-bound check. Writes zoom first, derives normalized cursor position from engine bounds on KBM or markerPos otherwise, computes inverse-zoom pan delta, clamps pan to &#91;0,1-1/newZoom&#93;, writes pan and adjusts markerPos for clamp displacement. Division-by-zero and missing model/data values are not repaired. Requests the hashed engine pan operation with controller/x/y before setting the supplied model to {x=x,y=y}. Failure in the first request can prevent the model write. Captures Warzone pan/zoom and source horizontal layout, installs separate pan/zoom callbacks applying the exact horizontal transformation. A scoreboard-open subscription clears DisableZoomIn/Out then reasserts each when its trigger button flag is down. Closing the scoreboard has no reset path in this body. Captures Warzone pan/zoom and source vertical layout. Two subscriptions recompute the displayed row top/bottom positions for pan or zoom delivery using the exact retained expressions; it does not write map state. Computes sqrt(x*x+y*y), divides both supplied table coordinates by it and returns the same table. Zero-length inputs are not guarded. An existing _ignoreInputTimer returns false. Otherwise creates/attaches a timer and returns true; on invocation clears the timer field then sets the supplied model to the captured value. No explicit close call is present in the owned callback. Reads currentWeaponIndex. The recovered body calls the engine selector with 1 only within the current-index==0 path, then assigns a local 0 without calling it. Do not invent the missing reverse-selection call for index 1. With a weaponId and valid nonzero ammoType model value, returns the localized formatted text containing supplied label and ammo type. Otherwise localizes the supplied label alone. Reads hudItems.inventory.selectedItemName under the controller model and injects its get value into localization of the supplied string key. Checks the shared SplitterWidgetElement. If present returns whether its state is Open; absent returns false. The controller argument is unused. Reads objectiveN.unlockProgress from the supplied model. Nonnil/nonnegative progress is injected into the description key; absent/negative progress localizes that key alone. Reads weaponN.hasAttachments from WarzoneInventory and returns equality to numeric 1. Read f383_arg0.weaponId or0. In order, request HeaderWeapon:setText with Engine hash_4F9F1239CFD921FE("WZ/WEAPON_WITH_ID", tostring(id+1)), then HeaderWeaponEquipped:setText with the same provider and literal "WZ/WEAPON_EQUIPED", tostring(id+1). These are numbered-label text requests, with no direct weapon-name or ammunition lookup in this selected body. Localization, text rendering, input errors and provider effects remain unverified.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:124` (original lines 744-746, 744-746): Mutates and returns the supplied array. Starting at its length while >=2, chooses math.random(currentLength), swaps that entry with the last current entry and decrements the bound. RNG implementation and seed are external. Reads surveyId from current-player AAR stats. A zero match-ID returns false,false. Otherwise creates/updates lobbyRoot.lastMatchID before duplicate checking. Repeated IDs return false,false. surveyId 0 returns a random survey-chance result,false for quitters, else false,true; a nonzero surveyId returns true,false. Missing stat structure returns false,true. The first argument is unused in this span.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:130` (original lines 979-992): Reads the supplied element model and forwards controller/model to FreeCursor.setupContext only when the model exists. Stores customGetHeight as an owned closure calling GetHeightByLocalTopBottom with the captured original element and later callback argument. Installation does not calculate a height. Gets mouse focus. If no menu was supplied but focus exists, uses focus:getMenu(). If a menu and detailed-view timer exist, closes the timer then clears its field. Uses a 1250-unit open timer. An already-open prompt cancels the menu close timer if present; a closed prompt creates an open timer when absent or resets it otherwise. On open-timer invocation it shows the prompt, marks open, closes and clears the open timer. Retain exact addElement and visibility requests in the trace; timer delivery itself is unresolved. Only an explicitly true open flag schedules closure. It creates a one-unit timer on the menu if absent, else resets that existing timer. On delivery clears the open flag, closes and clears the menu close-timer. This body does not invent cancellation of a still-pending open timer. Registers a ChatGlobal.MenuChatInActiveMode subscription whose closure hides the supplied widget on delivery without checking model value.

MAX_WOUNDS and opaque VOIP bound select child counts. PC+controller branch creates or reuses ControllerTypeSubscription, unsubscribes its old models, then subscribes ControllerType to refresh bindings. When the stored per-controller subscription element is nil, create it and assign the helper function to local slot f686_local8. That helper reads its supplied model, resolves value-1 through getpredictedclientnum, and requests predictedClientModel:set(getmodelforclient(resolved value)). The playerSpawned wrapper12577..12581 checks deadSpectator.playerIndex:get()~=nil, then calls the CURRENT value of captured local slot f686_local8. It does not save a function snapshot: later statements overwrite that slot with showPickupHint, heroHoldProgress, useHoldProgress, useTimerProgress and finally the voipInfo creation result12641. The numeric-for variable12618 shadows that slot rather than overwriting it. The playerIndex subscription12582 passes the currently bound function value as its callback argument, which differs from the wrapper lookup on delivery. A later playerSpawned refresh is therefore not guaranteed; current-value callability, model __call/error behavior and delivery/reentrancy timing remain unknown. The element reference is stored in the global table and no explicit close/free appears here; actual provider subscription lifetime remains unverified. iszombiesgame and CoD.isZombie gate unrelated extra model creation; PS4/PC truth values are not invented. Signed-in profile branch creates brightnessPreviewColor and reads colorblindMode; an unused recovered local reader refers to global profileVarName rather than its argument, so do not promote it to a valid invoked profile contract. After the loop PartyPrivacy is requested, global disabled/spinner/start/locked-in defaults are set, hideWorldForStreamer is initialized only if absent, and pregame/platform/storage/Demonware notifications are created. Creation of notification models is not backend delivery or persistence proof.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:097` (original lines 1034-1037, 1034-1037): Selected ui/main local bodies cover vehicle/reticle/lock-on replacement, common HUD hooks, spectator HUD, menu delivery and kill-stat updates only. Vehicle replacement may reuse prior type on force, skips same type or PC nonzero type unless forced, otherwise closes/resets filters/models before validating new type/moviecam/aerialcam; it resolves dynamic HUD factory, sets models, force-close ownership and menuOpened. Reticle replacement has same-type early exit and guarded dynamic class, wraps close to clear only same current object, then reevaluates visibility; lock-on always closes old, resets status/percent and uses defaultWidth for both axes literally. Common hooks always register common handlers and MP only when isZombie==false. SpawnSelectKillcam is multiplayer/non-demo, retaining REG4 receiver artifact; killcam HUD gets persistent force-close ownership. Spectator setup returns in demo, otherwise subscribes team once, creates WZ or generic CodCaster, initializes/focuses/one-shot postload and close unsubscription; non-spectator closes/clears it. In-game menu events queue before first snapshot, resolve xhash names through menu then overlay scans, reject duplicate pause/start menus/migration, update pause flags and ModelToUse, reuse startMenu or open menu/popup/overlay, subscribe close/state/animation and normally clear ModelToUse. PC WZ spectator quit early return can bypass final global clear; recovered gameScore reindexing remains literal. Last two selected callbacks write attacker/victim kills and guarded existing scoreboard killStats fields from unvalidated event data. Unselected registration/loader bodies and actual delivery stay open.

It performs no ownership change or online fetch itself. Takes controller, property key and expected value; returns the equality of CoD.perController&#91;controller&#93;&#91;key&#93; and the expected value. The nested table is indexed without a nil guard. Takes an unused first formal and controller as the second formal. Starts from opaque Enum&#91;@"groupmemberstatus"&#93;&#91;@"hash_A04B5BF30F4F831"&#93;, whose value and equality to group_member_status_owner are unproved; if perController&#91;secondFormal&#93;.selectedGroup is truthy, replaces it with getselectedgroupmemberstatus(getxuid64(secondFormal)). Returns equality with group_member_status_owner. The selected stopped caller supplies only one explicit argument, so this source contract preserves the absent second argument instead of moving the first into it. Per-controller indexing remains unguarded. Takes an unused element and controller. Returns true in frontend, then true for a shoutcaster. Otherwise obtains scoreboardInfo.muteButtonPromptVisible from that controller's model and returns true only when the model exists and its value is false/nil; absent model returns false. It only chooses visibility; it does not mute a player. Returns true when loc_language:get() equals language_korean and false otherwise. The dvar and enum values are external inputs; no language setting is changed.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:179` (original lines 923-936, 923-936): Constructs the scorestreak-ready notification and progress ring. ScorestreakInfo width changes scale the parent; non-nil heroHoldProgress updates ring rendering. The ring Visible predicate is AlwaysTrue, controller text distinguishes PC gamepad, and parent states distinguish caster/PC. Exact model/input subscriptions, close and optional PostLoadFunc precede return self. Ability readiness and native event production remain separate contracts.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:105` (original lines 1034-1037, 1034-1037): Selected model wrappers create/init controller models; InitIfNotSet checks model existence, not its stored false/nil value. GetMapValue hashes name and returns existing nonnil field else supplied default; AlreadyRegistered is identity. LocalizeIfNotCodcaster localizes only outside assigned/codcaster. MessageDialog subscription replaces the previous one, conditionally closes an existing dialog, opens an in-game HUD/controller-qualified priority overlay and locks input, or frontend overlay when unoccluded; close callback clears _messageDialogBox. HoldFinished returns true for absent/nil model, else tests the exact bit. MenuPreservesButton writes a map. PlayClipAndThenClose registers clip_over after playClip, with no cancellation fallback. CustomElementFunction calls only a truthy named field with four exact args. PlayMusic forwards to Engine. SelfShowsBuildInfo increments the global model and restores its captured prior value on close; nil/arithmetic/concurrent-close hazards are retained.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:269` (original lines 1034-1037): Rejects vehicle, remote-control or view-only-linked players. Applies team-owner-specific objective flag gates unless the arg2/arg3/objective override holds; only then forwards objId and predicted client number to objectiveisplayerusing. Rejects demos and non-Warzone/nonfull inventory; otherwise tests ALSO_ALLOW_SWAP in HUDItems.weapon3dHintState, defaulting that value to0. Preserves the exact direct source forwarding contract: return CoD.WaypointUtility.ConvertToColorInternal( f110_local0 .. "_Base", f110_local0 ). Other ordered reads/writes below occur before this return; callee/native effects and missing-input error behavior remain external. Preserves the exact direct source forwarding contract: return CoD.WaypointUtility.ConvertToColorInternal( f111_local0 .. "_Muted", f111_local0 ). Other ordered reads/writes below occur before this return; callee/native effects and missing-input error behavior remain external. Preserves the exact direct source forwarding contract: return CoD.WaypointUtility.ConvertToColorInternal( f112_local0 .. "_Bright", f112_local0 ). Other ordered reads/writes below occur before this return; callee/native effects and missing-input error behavior remain external. Lazily maps meansofdeathlist bundle entries into enum-indexed rows, caches even an empty table and returns the table by identity. Compares g_gametype dvar with each ipairs entry of the vararg table; returns true on equal entry and false after iteration. The n field records vararg count but is not used by ipairs. Returns true for absent/falsy roundsplayed or numeric0; otherwise false. Defaults ratio1, divides ammoInClip get() or1 by clipMaxAmmo get() or1 only when both fields exist, then returns ratio,0,0,0. Numeric0 denominator remains truthy and is not guarded/clamped. Requests compass corners/north. Nonzero north-x swaps normalized x/y outputs; else nonzero north-y keeps them aligned. If both north components zero returns nil,nil. Does not guard corner denominators or clamp. Requests corners/north and interpolates world x/y with swapped relative coordinates for nonzero north-x, unswapped for nonzero north-y. Both zero yields nil,nil; no clamp is added. Clamps interpolation amount0..1 then requests component-wise RGB interpolation on the supplied receiver. Saves original clip_over handler then installs a wrapper. On delivered clip_over with no next clip and nondefault state, EnemyKilled decrements a numeric kill counter and either replays DefaultClip or sets DefaultState; other nondefault states reset. Calls originalClipOver last without a nil guard. Sets the nested prematch timer first. False show flag hides numbers and returns; otherwise if start time exists, >5s delay installs faction_start and a one-shot timer, <=5s plays Start immediately. Finally starts the countdown numbers clip. No explicit timer cancellation/close hook appears. Gets predicted client number, concatenates GetClientClantag then GetClientName, and returns it. No separator or nil guard is added. Truthy _allowingButtonRepeats returns without native call; otherwise marks true before the native push request. This is a flag, not a local reference count. Falsy flag returns; otherwise clears it before native pop request. Failure rollback is not provided. Lazily builds the set of written weapon names plus current gameMode suffix, then returns the slot selected by SafeGetModelValue(viewmodelWeaponName). The none/killstreak_remote table is defined but unused; cache is not rebuilt when gameMode changes here. Only existing hints table and truthy indexed entry return the localized entry. Missing paths assign hash0 then fall through without returning it. When abilityHintIndex model/value exists, returns hints-table and #hints==expected. Missing paths assign the none enum then fall through without an explicit return; retain GagdetHintIndex spelling. Returns when Warzone UI is hidden. Requires console, message and primary client; computes names/colors and localized message. Console path writes line fields with reversed-language ordering (missing secondary text is not cleared in the reversed branch), then forces subscriptions. PC path shows a formatted game event using secondary player when present, otherwise primary. No server callout generation or log persistence is proved.

- `hmh07-main:002` (original lines 1034-1037, 1034-1037): Registration order: register hud_force_kill_killstreak_hud using current f0_local3 value; read menu owner and use primary-controller native only when owner==nil (false does not fall back). Make one refresh callback capturing menu/controller, ignoring its own argument and requesting HUD_UpdateRefresh(menu,{controller=owner}). Subscribe that same callback to ten UIVisibilityBit model paths. Subscribe selecting-location bit with a separate closure requesting current f0_local4(menu,{controller=owner}); subscribe heldWeapons&#91;0&#93;:create(viewmodelWeaponName) with callback requesting HUDUtility.updateicepick(menu,menu.safeArea,model:get(),owner). If team model absent, create and set it0. Only under CoD.isPC, register chooseclass_hotkey using HUD_Handle_ChooseClass_HotKey, then subscribe LastInput: when hashed native gate truthy, request HideScoreboard and then set MultiItemPickup status through the exact hashed enum via ModelUtility helper. Finally create/reset forceScoreboard to0 regardless of PC. There are no subscription-handle/nil/field guards or cleanup/unsubscribe here; multiple calls can repeat registrations and reset models. Builds icon/glow, controller prompts, cycle prompt, hold-to-activate variants, LED and stack count. Prompt HiddenPreference delegates Warzone hide settings; Hidden checks killcam/play-of-match/team-spectator; the non-WZ KBM ability/ultimate/streak variants are explicitly AlwaysFalse, while KeyboardAndMouseAbilityWZ allows mouse/keyboard. Cycle Visible requires profile warzoneCycleItems=1 and quick inventory open, with Hidden AlwaysTrue fallback. Settings/storage/inventory/input notifications reevaluate those states. Stack text binds equipmentStackCount; Invisible when count<1. Self Hidden=count<1; InUse=state inuse; Deployed=AlwaysFalse; EmptyCharging=charging with powerRatio0; Charging=charging; PowerBased=not hidden. powerRatio/state/count updates reevaluate self. Through the named setState override, pulse only if ShouldPulseNewAmmo and current PowerBased; ammo link repeats that predicate, id link fades in only for cycle-items+quick inventory. PC alpha helper runs immediately and on powerRatio/hideRadiationHold. PreLoadFunc is called near the END, after close hook/subscriptions; IsPC hides the gamepad hold child. Return self. Frozen span contains no full class clips/close body outside it; preserve them as precise dependencies, not assumed implementation. Native event delivery, inventory production and settings remain external.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:050` (original lines 1034-1037, 1034-1037): Reuse stopped common/character/preview/XP caller facts: several ZM-named children are constructed unconditionally in shared recovered source, so names alone do not exclude these calls. Difficulty image only selects the four-entry difficulty array for LAN zclassic outside offline custom; otherwise transparent. Localized map name has LAN tutorial/trial special cases then generic map fallback. Hide preview resets three widget paths to CoD.NOTHING, shown/info false and item fields empty/hash0. IsOfflineCustom compares initialized model to CUSTOM_GAME. Preview index getter conditionally resolves item name/desc/image, localizes xhash name and forwards info. Three show helpers select current map story, set respective widget path and shown true. Lobby-client subscription is added only if no saved subscription, rebuilding the ZM character list on membersChanged. IsDoubleNP excludes in-game tutorial, then checks native zombies event, else dvar>1. Its supplied XP-widget visibility also requires IsZombies/live, so that edge is only conditionally excluded given an original mode proof, not globally excluded here. No unrelated Zombies gameplay or backend grants are reconstructed.

The owned provider, only when invoked, builds image model rows from each supplied item whose chosen field exists, using current session mode and item-image requests. Passing the provider is distinct from executing it. Returns 0 offline. Online, scans all balance items for matching name, keeps the last match, subtracts a positive supplied adjustment and clamps below zero. It reads the AARPreMatch baseline when that field exists and returns the nonnegative difference. Recovered f59_local1..4 assignments lack local declarations here; no lexical repair is made. Returns without changes when Black Market is disabled. Otherwise loops the selected stream tier items, separates signature-weapon/outfit/gesture properties, rewrites weaponRef/rarity/unlocked/isLootCase, clears moved fields and appends models/properties rows unless the optional name filter rejects them. The local bribe-name predicate is defined then called immediately for each item; it is not a deferred service callback. The optional rarity dvar overrides the computed rarity. The fake-tier path returns currentTier minus three and 200 when the needed model exists. Otherwise uses AARPreMatch tiers/credits, replacing tiers with currentSeasonTiers when present; absent baseline data delegates to GetLootStreamData. Only two values are returned. Fake-tier mode returns currentTier, 800, lootTierMax and 1 when the current-tier model exists. Normal data returns currentTier/currentLootXP/lootTierMax and redeemed skips or 0. A missing progress/currentTier path returns four zeroes. Other child lookups in a present path are not individually guarded. Returns false outside public online play or with Black Market disabled. Otherwise the forced AAR overlay or LootCaseRewardUp returns true; the remaining path compares prematch tier against current tier. It does not grant rewards.
